Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统化的水下图像重建评估流程，用于评估重建方法的准确性、跨相机运动的重建一致性以及水参数的影响。通过该流程对比了基于显式物理散射模型的传统方法与视觉语言模型（VLMs），发现VLMs在各项评估中全面且显著优于物理模型，主要归因于强大的图像先验能力。真实水下场景的结果进一步验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像恢复旨在去除水体影响，但截至目前该领域的评估缺乏系统性，不同方法之间难以进行公平、全面的比较。作者希望建立一个统一的评估流程，以量化评估不同重建方法的性能差异，并探究当前主流方法（物理模型方法与VLMs）之间的优劣。

Method: 作者设计了一个系统化的评估流程，包含三个维度：1）重建精度评估；2）相机移动下重建的一致性评估；3）水参数变化对重建效果的影响评估。利用该流程对两类方法进行对比：一类是基于显式但近似的物理散射模型构建的方法，另一类是未受显式物理模型训练的视觉语言模型（VLMs）。

Result: 评估结果显示，视觉语言模型（VLMs）在所有评估指标上全面且显著地优于基于物理模型的方法。作者推测这一优势主要来自于VLMs强大的图像先验能力。真实水下场景图像的结果也对这一结论提供了强有力的支持。

Conclusion: 水下图像重建中，缺乏显式物理模型的视觉语言模型凭借其强大的图像先验，能够大幅超越基于物理散射模型的方法。这表明在复杂的水下成像条件下，学习到的图像先验比近似的物理建模更为有效，为该领域的未来研究方向提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Underwater image restoration consists of recovering an image which looks like there is no water present. To date, evaluation has not been systematic. This paper describes a systematic evaluation pipeline for underwater reconstruction, which can be used to assess a method for accuracy; consistency of reconstruction over camera moves; and the effect of water parameters. We use this pipeline to evaluate a range of current procedures, from models constructed using explicit but approximate physical models of scattering to Vision-Language Models (VLMs which are not currently trained with explicit physical models). Overall, VLMs wholly and significantly outperform physically based models in our evaluation, likely because of the importance of a strong image prior. Results on images of real underwater scenes strongly confirm the evaluation.

</details>


### [10] [TangPoetryBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark and Rubric-Conditioned Evaluator for Poetry-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11452)
*Haoqi Hu,Tongji Luo,Li Zhang,Boning Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文生图模型在诗歌插画任务上的评估难题，提出TangPoetryBench多维度基准（320首唐诗×4个模型，10个维度人工标注）和PoemAutoEvaluator（PAE）评估器，后者与强专有评估器（Claude）达到同等水平，可泛化到未见生成器及宋词，并允许基准无需新人工标注即可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型越来越多被要求绘制文学与文化内容，但现有指标（CLIPScore、BLIPScore、VQAScore）只奖励字面图文对应，无法衡量插图是否成功，更无法测量诗歌隐含意象与情感等最深层次需求，甚至无法区分最好与最差的模型。

Method: 构建TangPoetryBench基准：320首古典唐诗×4个SOTA文生图模型共1280张图像，在10个维度上进行质量控制的人工标注；基于此数据提出PoemAutoEvaluator（PAE），一个开放、基于评分标准的评估器。

Result: 揭示了当前文生图模型在诗歌插图任务上共性与模型特定的优缺点（包括唤起诗歌隐含情感的能力）；PAE与强专有评估器Claude达到同等水平，能泛化到未见生成器和第二种诗歌传统（宋词），使基准可在无新人工标注情况下扩展。

Conclusion: 研究发布基准、标注和评估器，为文生图模型在诗歌插画等文化与文学内容生成任务上提供了更全面的多维度评估框架，弥补了现有指标无法捕捉隐含情感与意象的不足。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models are increasingly asked to illustrate literary and cultural content, yet we cannot measure how well an image renders the meaning of a poem. The task is many-sided: a good illustration must be visually sound, faithful to the poem's imagery and scene, culturally and stylistically apt, free of spurious text, and true to its emotion, and its deepest requirements, imagery and especially implicit emotion, are never stated in the words. Existing metrics (CLIPScore, BLIPScore, VQAScore) reward literal text-image correspondence and so cannot tell whether an illustration succeeds, let alone why, or even separate the best model from the worst. We introduce TangPoetryBench, a multi-dimensional benchmark of 1,280 images (320 classical Chinese Tang poems x 4 state-of-the-art T2I models) with quality-controlled human annotations across ten dimensions. Analyzing this data, we reveal the shared and model-specific strengths and weaknesses of current T2I models, including their ability to evoke a poem's implicit emotion. We further introduce PoemAutoEvaluator (PAE), an open, rubric-conditioned evaluator that reaches parity with a strong proprietary judge (Claude), generalizes to an unseen generator and a second poetic tradition (Song Ci), and lets the benchmark scale to new images without fresh human annotation. We release the benchmark, annotations, and evaluator.

</details>


### [11] [Multi-Agent Target-Existence Verification and Learned Mask Geometry Refinement: Winning Report of the MeViS-Text Track at the 8th LSVOS Challenge 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11458)
*Jungyoon Lee,Gyuil Lim,Doeon Kim,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSUPER系统，是LSVOS挑战赛2026 MeViS-Text赛道的第一名方案，用于基于文本运动表达的视频目标分割（包括欺骗性无目标表达）。系统采用三异构多模态大语言模型独立推理加单一综合验证的架构，并进一步将存在性验证解耦为独立的多智能体审计，以处理难以判别的无目标负样本。最终在官方排行榜上取得0.9081339614的最终得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeViS-Text任务的核心挑战在于表达式可能包含欺骗性无目标表达（即视频中不存在匹配对象，应输出空掩码）。验证集上系统能拒绝所有无目标表达，但测试集上仍有大量难负样本未被识别——这些硬负样本描述了一个看似合理的目标，仅在完整时间谓词下才失效。此外，部分剩余错误属于掩码几何与标注风格不匹配的风格性误差，而非语义误差。

Method: SSUPER管道分为多个阶段：(1) 语义解析：将每个表达式解析为视觉概念；(2) 候选生成：使用SAM 3.1生成全视频候选masklet；(3) 目标ID选择。每个推理阶段由三个异构多模态大语言模型独立执行相同提示，再经单一综合传递提交经模式验证的结论。针对难负样本问题，将存在性验证解耦为独立的多智能体审计，全面核对谓词（类别、数量、动作、轨迹、事件顺序、语义角色），区分"目标不存在"与"暂时不可见"两种情况，折扣相机运动造成的表观运动，并要求矛盾证据（而非仅不确定性）才判定为无目标。最后，一个仅用训练数据的StyleRefiner将掩码几何与MeViSv2标注风格对齐，同时保证不改变任何存在性判定。

Result: 系统在验证集上拒绝了所有无目标表达。解耦的存在性审计在无需任何新分割调用的情况下恢复了大部分残余无目标错误。最终系统在官方挑战排行榜上取得0.9081339614的Final分数，位列第一名。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：将"存在性判断"与"目标选择"解耦为独立的审计流程能有效处理硬负样本；在语义已确定的前提下，部分剩余误差本质上是风格性的而非语义性的。三模型异构冗余加综合验证的架构以及"要求矛盾证据"的保守判定策略，为视频目标分割中的语义推理提供了一种可靠范式。

Abstract: We present the first-place solution to the MeViS-Text track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge 2026: referring video object segmentation guided by written motion expressions, including deceptive no-target expressions that match no object in the video and must yield empty masks in every frame. Our pipeline, SSUPER, resolves each expression into a visual concept, generates full-video candidate masklets with SAM~3.1, and selects target IDs. At every reasoning stage, three heterogeneous multimodal large language models independently execute the same stage-specific prompt before a single synthesis pass commits one schema-validated verdict. Although this system rejects every no-target expression in validation, the leaderboard reveals that a substantial share of test no-target cases still slips through. The reason is that hard negatives name a plausible object and fail only under the complete temporal predicate, so when selection and existence are decided together, a category-plausible masklet anchors the verdict. Hence, we decouple existence verification into an independent multi-agent audit of the full predicate (category, count, action, trajectory, event order, and semantic role) that distinguishes absence from temporary invisibility, discounts apparent motion caused by camera movement, and requires contradicting evidence rather than mere uncertainty for a no-target verdict. Without any new segmentation call, this audit recovers most of the residual no-target errors. A training-data-only StyleRefiner then aligns mask geometry with the annotation style of MeViSv2 while preserving every presence decision by construction, showing that once the semantics are fixed, part of the remaining error is stylistic rather than semantic. The complete system reaches a Final score of 0.9081339614 on the official challenge leaderboard.

</details>


### [12] [Gaussian Meta-Space Augmentation for Stacking Ensembles in Multimodal IPMN Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11472)
*Max A. Nelson,Eminenur Sen Tasci,Zhixiang Wang,Zongwei Zhou,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea M. Bejar,Elif Keles,Ziliang Hong,Sıtkı Safa Taflan,Muhammed Enes Tasci,Frank H. Miller,Michael B. Wallace,Rajesh N. Keswani,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出cUPMI（类条件高斯增强的组合器对数概率元特征方法），用于胰腺导管内乳头状黏液性肿瘤（IPMN）的异型增生风险分层预测。该方法在多中心分析中验证，将影像组学、2.5D CNN等多流特征与多序列MRI及解剖分解信息融合，通过正则化集成堆叠组合器实现风险预测。结果显示cUPMI对高容量树组合器有稳定增益，而fold-locked融合的RF堆叠模型达到最优性能（QWK 0.595, AUC 0.839）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胰腺癌是最致命的恶性肿瘤之一，IPMN的风险分层对早期干预至关重要，但传统方法依赖侵入性组织活检。基于视觉的方法（影像组学、深度学习）虽有潜力但彼此独立，且多序列MRI和解剖分解信息未被有效融合。作者旨在通过有效的特征融合框架提升IPMN序数型异型增生风险的预测性能。

Method: 提出cUPMI方法，即类条件高斯增强（class-conditional Gaussian augmentation），对集成堆叠组合器的对数概率元特征进行增强。融合多流信息包括：影像组学、2.5D CNN、3D DenseNet-121等深度学习流，多序列MRI（T1W/T2W），以及胰腺解剖分解（头、体、尾）分析。采用精细正则化和校准的集成堆叠组合器（如RF、XGBoost、L2-logistic）进行序数IPMN风险预测。

Result: 多中心分析发现：(1) cUPMI对L2-logistic二分类堆叠增益有限；(2) 对高容量树组合器在二分类和仅影像组学设置中一致正则化（RF +0.015, XGBoost +0.024 AUC）；(3) 在8流影像组学XGBoost序数任务中增益最明显（3类 +0.022 QWK）；(4) fold-locked融合影像组学与2.5D CNN流的RF堆叠为最强模型：QWK 0.595（95% CI [0.54, 0.64]），二分类AUC 0.839，超越所有基线。

Conclusion: cUPMI作为一种类条件高斯增强方法，能有效正则化高容量树组合器，尤其对序数风险预测任务带来稳定增益。fold-locked的多流融合策略（影像组学+2.5D CNN）结合RF堆叠可实现最先进的IPMN风险分层性能，展示了多模态特征融合在胰腺癌早期干预中的潜力。

Abstract: Pancreatic cancer is among the most lethal malignancies; risk stratification of intraductal papillary mucinous neoplasms (IPMNs) offers a crucial opportunity for early intervention but typically requires invasive tissue biopsy. Dominant vision-based approaches, including radiomics and deep learning, provide promising but initially separate discrimination opportunities. Similarly, multisequence MRI (T1W/T2W) and anatomically decomposed (head, body and tail) analysis of the pancreas provide additional and potentially complementary signals. Effective fusion of this information is crucial in ordinal IPMN dysplasia risk prediction and can be accomplished via a meticulously regularized and calibrated ensemble stacking combiner. We present cUPMI, a class-conditional Gaussian augmentation of a combiner's log-probability meta-features, and test it on various prediction paradigms. In our multi-center analysis, we find cUPMI adds limited value to properly regularized L2-logistic binary classification stacks, but consistently regularizes higher-capacity tree combiners in the binary and radiomics-only setting (RF +0.015 and XGBoost +0.024 binary AUC, positive in all seeds). Its cleanest ordinal benefit appears for XGBoost on an 8-stream radiomics task (3-class no < low < high, +0.022 QWK in all seeds). Separately, fold-locked fusion of radiomics and 2.5D CNN streams yields the strongest overall model, an RF stack reaching QWK 0.595 (95% CI [0.54, 0.64]) and binary AUC 0.839, surpassing radiomics, 2.5D ResNet, and 3D DenseNet-121 baselines.

</details>


### [13] [Test-Time Hallucination Control in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11474)
*Mehran Tamjidi,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Ali Cheraghian,Mohammadreza Alimoradijazi,Aijun An,Hossein Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TTH（Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation）的训练无关方法，通过零样本多模态分类器生成辅助logits，在token级别融合以缓解大视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）存在物体幻觉问题，生成关于输入图像的非事实内容，严重阻碍其实际应用可靠性。现有训练方法成本高（需要大量计算资源和数据），而训练无关方法要么需要多次解码轮次增加计算开销，要么以模型特定的方式修改内部状态，有损害预训练知识的风险。

Method: 提出Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation (TTH)方法：引入token验证器模块，实现为零样本多模态分类器（MMC），生成基于输入图像的辅助logits，然后在token级别与原始LVLM输出融合（针对候选池中的物体token），采用基于熵的加权方案实现稳健准确的预测。

Result: 在多个LVLM家族和不同基准上的大量实验表明，TTH持续提高了准确性和稳健性，证明了其泛化性和实际有效性。

Conclusion: TTH作为一种训练无关方法，同时解决了计算开销大和模型特定内部修改的两个局限，展现出色的泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Object Hallucination in large vision-language models (LVLMs), where models generate non-factual content about input images, remains a critical barrier to their reliability in real-world applications. Existing mitigation strategies can be categorized into training-based and training-free methods. Training-based methods often achieve strong performance but are costly, requiring extensive computational resources, large-scale data, and time-consuming fine-tuning. Training-free approaches are particularly appealing due to their efficiency. However, existing training-free methods either require multiple decoding rounds, which adds computational overhead, or modify internal states in a model-specific way that risks degrading pretrained knowledge. We propose Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation (TTH) method, a novel training-free method that addresses both limitations. TTH introduces a token-validator module, implemented as a zero-shot Multi-Modal Classifier (MMC), to generate auxiliary logits grounded in the input image. These logits are fused with the original LVLM outputs at the token level for object tokens selected from a candidate pool. An entropy-based weighting scheme is then applied to enable robust and accurate predictions. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLM families and diverse benchmarks demonstrate that TTH consistently improves accuracy and robustness, underscoring its generalizability and practical effectiveness. Code is released at https://github.com/Mehran-TAM/TTH

</details>


### [14] [Language-Structured Relational Q-Learning for Threat-Aware Control in Safety-Critical Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11498)
*Aditya Humnabadkar,Huaizhong Zhang,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语言结构化关系Q学习（ERQ-Net），在训练时利用自然语言描述周围车辆行为，但推理时隐藏语言信息，迫使策略仅从可观测运动学与交互中推断威胁相关性。在2500个安全关键场景中，语言结构化训练将测试成功率从49-52%提升至55-58%，并将对抗目标注意力从1.2x提升至2.1x，显示出突发的威胁感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自然语言的场景生成提供了一种直观描述罕见复杂驾驶交互的方式，但尚不确定语言结构化数据训练是否能真正带来自适应的控制策略。

Method: 提出Language-Structured Relational Q-Learning，通过Ego-Centric Relational Q-Network（ERQ-Net）实现，从动态交通图中联合学习车辆间相关性与动作价值。训练时使用语言描述周围车辆行为，但提示与语义角色对策略隐藏，ERQ-Net须仅从可观测运动学和交互中推断威胁相关性。

Result: 在2500个安全关键场景中，语言结构化训练将测试成功率从49-52%提升至55-58%，对抗目标注意力从1.2x提升至2.1x。然而策略表现与最佳恒定动作相当，简单策略组合可解决76%的场景；奖励重加权和边际整形无法消除这种"识别-控制差距"导致的策略崩溃。

Conclusion: 语言结构化训练能显著提升威胁识别能力，但这种表征增益无法稳定转化为自适应控制，存在识别-控制差距。文章还评估了真实性、关键性、语义准确性以及到CARLA的状态接口表征迁移，揭示了语言结构化关系策略学习在安全关键驾驶场景中的优势与局限。

Abstract: Natural-language-based scenario generation offers an intuitive means of describing rare and complex driving interactions, yet it is still uncertain whether training with language-structured data leads to truly adaptive control policies. We propose Language-Structured Relational Q-Learning, instantiated through an Ego-Centric Relational Q-Network (ERQ-Net), which jointly learns inter-vehicle relevance and action values from dynamic traffic graphs. Language descriptions define surrounding-vehicle behaviours during training, while prompts and semantic actor roles are hidden from the policy. ERQ-Net must therefore infer threat relevance solely from observable kinematics and interactions. Across 2,500 safety-critical scenarios, language-structured training improves test success from 49-52% to 55-58% and increases adversary-focused attention from 1.2x to 2.1x, demonstrating emergent threat awareness. However, this representational gain does not consistently translate into adaptive control: trained policies perform similarly to the best constant action, while a portfolio of simple policies solves 76% of scenarios. We formalise this discrepancy as a recognition-control gap and show that reward reweighting and margin shaping do not eliminate the resulting policy collapse. Evaluations of realism, criticality, semantic accuracy, and transfer of state-interface representations to CARLA further highlight both the strengths and the constraints of language-structured relational policy learning in safety-critical driving scenarios.

</details>


### [15] [New Orthogonal Multiwavelet Filters Derived by Matrix Spectral Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11518)
*Vasil Kolev,Todor Cooklev,Fritz Keinert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过快速Bauer矩阵谱分解方法，基于正交CL多小波滤波器构造了两种具有超紧致支撑的新型正交多小波。新多小波具备正交性、对称/反对称性，其中一种在编码和平滑性上优于其他超紧致多小波。在子带边缘检测、灰度/彩色图像压缩及1D/2D信号去噪中与GHM、SA4、CL、Integer Haar和Alpert等多滤波器进行了比较，结果表明新多小波在SSIM和MS-SSIM等人类视觉指标上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超紧致多小波在编码效率、平滑性和视觉质量方面存在不足，需要构造兼具正交性、对称性且性能更优的新型多小波滤波器，以满足图像压缩和信号去噪等应用需求。

Method: 采用快速Bauer方法对正交CL多小波滤波器的矩阵乘积滤波器进行矩阵谱分解，构造两种新型正交多小波，并评估其在边缘检测、图像压缩和信号去噪中的性能。

Result: 新构造的多小波具有正交性、对称/反对称性和超紧致支撑，其中一种在编码和平滑性上优于现有超紧致多小波。在图像压缩和去噪应用中，新多小波在SSIM、MS-SSIM等人类视觉质量指标上优于GHM、SA4、CL、Integer Haar和Alpert等多滤波器。

Conclusion: 所构造的两种新型超紧致正交多小波在图像压缩和信号去噪等应用中展现出更优的视觉质量表现，为多小波在图像处理领域的应用提供了更好的滤波器选择。

Abstract: The paper considers the construction of two new orthogonal multiwavelets with supercompact support by using the Fast Bauer's method for matrix spectral factorization on the matrix product filter of the orthogonal CL multiwavelet filter. The new multiwavelets possess orthogonality, symmetry/antisymmetry, and one of them provides better coding and smoothness than other supercompact multiwavelets.
  The performance of the new multiwavelet filters in subband-based edge detection, grayscale and color image compression and 1D and 2D signal denoising is compared with the GHM, SA4, CL, Integer Haar and Alpert multifilters. The comparative analysis shows that new multiwavelets can provides better human visual measures, SSIM and MS-SSIM in image compression and denoising applications.

</details>


### [16] [Generative Semantic Segmentation via an Observable Semantic-Image Interface and Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11537)
*Weize Cai,Yongqi Dong,Zhida Shao,Zixin Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Semantic Prism，一种条件语义图像生成与细化的确定性推理框架。通过扩散蒸馏的单步生成器渲染语义RGB图像，利用像素颜色与固定类色码本的逐像素距离构建显式概率接口，并引入分层生成器证据对齐（HGEA）预测接口logit空间的加性残差。同时提出上下文接口-层级不一致（C-IHD）无需辅助预测器即可排序剩余像素误差。在Cityscapes上达到72.07% mIoU，比直接接口解码高11.39个百分点，ECE仅0.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语义分割将结构化预测渲染为图像，但直接颜色解码易受颜色漂移和边界混合影响，而潜在特征解码器预测独立输出分布又可能使渲染图像沦为中间可视化，缺乏显式的概率接口。现有方法难以同时保持图像定义的接口与精细的分布预测。

Method: 1) 使用扩散蒸馏的一步生成器渲染语义RGB图像；2) 计算渲染颜色到固定类颜色码本的逐像素距离，构建显式概率接口；3) 提出分层生成器证据对齐（HGEA），对齐多层级生成器特征，用零初始化的输出投影预测接口logit空间的加性残差；4) 提出上下文接口-层级不一致（C-IHD），利用接口与细化分布间的分歧作为固定读出器来排序像素误差。

Result: 在Cityscapes 500张验证集上达到72.07% mIoU，比直接接口解码高11.39个百分点，期望校准误差（ECE）仅0.41%；BDD100K上达到62.22% mIoU；在ACDC上跨域迁移（源冻结）达到46.89% mIoU。C-IHD在所有数据集上持续优于最大softmax概率的像素误差排序，ACDC上AUPR从0.6580提升到0.7557。

Conclusion: Semantic Prism证明保留图像定义的接口作为参考分布，同时用对齐的多层级特征预测残差进行细化，能同时获得高精度、良好校准性和无需额外模型即可实现的可靠不确定性排序。联合对齐多层级证据对性能有明显增益，且该方法具有较强的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative semantic segmentation exposes structured predictions as images, but direct color decoding is susceptible to color drift and boundary mixing, whereas latent-feature decoders that predict a separate output distribution may relegate the rendered image to an intermediate visualization. We present Semantic Prism, a conditional semantic-image generation-and-refinement framework with deterministic inference. A diffusion-distilled one-step generator renders a semantic RGB image; per-pixel distances from the rendered colors to a fixed class-color codebook define an explicit probabilistic interface. Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment spatially aligns multi-level generator features and uses a zero-initialized output projection to predict an additive residual in the interface logit space, retaining the image-defined interface as the reference for the final distribution. The interface and refined distributions further enable Contextual Interface--Hierarchy Disagreement (C-IHD), a fixed readout for ranking remaining pixel errors without an auxiliary predictor or additional forward pass. On the 500-image Cityscapes validation set, Semantic Prism achieves 72.07% mean intersection over union, 11.39 mIoU points above direct-interface decoding, with 0.41% expected calibration error. Matched-capacity ablations over three seeds support the benefit of jointly aligned multi-level evidence. A separately trained model attains 62.22% mIoU on BDD100K, while the Cityscapes-trained model reaches 46.89\% mIoU under source-frozen transfer to the Adverse Conditions Dataset with Correspondences, without target-domain adaptation. Across all three datasets, C-IHD consistently improves the area under the precision--recall curve for pixel-error ranking over maximum softmax probability on the same segmentation predictions; on ACDC, it raises AUPR from 0.6580 to 0.7557.

</details>


### [17] [Through Van Gogh's Eyes: Global Style Transfer with Diffusion Mod](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11546)
*Jeongha Lee,Yujin Kim,Ghazanfar Ali,Suhyun Kim,Jae-In Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全局风格迁移（GST）范式，以多对一方式聚合目标艺术家的多幅作品，将共享全局风格迁移到单张内容图像上。通过全局风格引导（GSG）在扩散模型的h空间学习残差风格偏移，以及内容对齐引导（CAG）保持内容语义结构，在WikiArt数据集上实现优于现有方法的风格保真度、内容保持和输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有艺术图像合成方法存在局限：传统风格迁移只针对单幅或少幅参考作品（一对多方式），无法捕捉艺术家整体风格分布；基于艺术家名字的文本到图像扩散模型受文本诱导偏差影响，仅能复现少数标志性作品的模式。因此需要一种能聚合多幅作品、学习艺术家级全局风格的新范式。

Method: 提出GST（全局风格迁移）范式，采用多对一方式聚合目标艺术家的多幅作品并将共享全局风格迁移到内容图像。核心包含两个组件：1）GSG（全局风格引导），在扩散模型的中间特征空间（h空间）学习残差全局风格偏移，在固定提示词下仅从视觉统计中学习艺术家级风格语义，避免文本依赖；2）CAG（内容对齐引导），一种免训练的感知引导机制，在允许艺术家特定几何形变的同时保持内容图像的语义结构。

Result: 在WikiArt数据集上的实验表明，GST在风格保真度、内容保持和输出多样性方面均优于现有风格迁移方法和基于扩散模型的艺术合成方法。

Conclusion: GST通过多对一的艺术图像合成范式有效解决了艺术家全局风格表征问题，GSG和CAG分别解决了风格学习与内容保持两大挑战，为艺术风格合成提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Artistic image synthesis aims to recreate the expressive visual identity of a target artist, yet existing methods often fail to capture an artist's global style. Conventional style transfer methods transfer the style of one or a few reference artworks to a content image in a One-to-One manner, making them effective for artwork-level stylization but limited in representing the broader stylistic distribution of an artist. Text-to-image diffusion models conditioned on artist names, such as '~ in Van Gogh style', offer greater flexibility, but they often suffer from text-induced bias and reproduce patterns from only a few iconic works. To address these limitations, we introduce Global Style Transfer (GST), an artistic image synthesis paradigm, in a Many-to-One manner, that aggregates multiple artworks from a target artist and transfers their shared global style to a single content image. For GST, we propose Global Style Guidance (GSG), which learns a residual global style offset in the intermediate feature space, or h-space, of a diffusion model under a fixed prompt. By learning artist-level style semantics purely from visual statistics, GSG mitigates text-dependent artistic bias. We further propose Content Alignment Guidance (CAG), a training-free perceptual guidance mechanism that preserves the semantic structure of the content image while allowing artist-specific geometric deformation. Experiments on WikiArt demonstrate that GST achieves superior stylistic fidelity, content preservation, and output diversity compared to existing style transfer and diffusion-based artistic synthesis methods.

</details>


### [18] [From Synthesis to Removal: Physics-Grounded Reflection Simulation and Diffusion-Based Video Dereflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11562)
*Zepeng Wang,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个闭环框架S2R，统一了物理驱动的反射视频合成(S2R-Synthesis)、基于扩散模型的视频反射去除(S2R-Removal)和基准评估(S2R-Bench)，用于解决视频反射去除这一长期被忽视的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单幅图像反射去除已被广泛研究，但视频反射去除因缺少成对视频数据、时间一致的去除模型和专用评估基准而研究不足。

Method: 1) S2R-Synthesis：在结构空间进行物理驱动的增强，并用训练的视频扩散渲染器生成成对的反射视频，建模粗糙模糊、厚度重影和反射率变化等玻璃效应；2) S2R-Removal：首个基于扩散的视频反射去除模型，通过反射感知的潜变量适配和一步像素-几何细化，在单次去噪步骤中恢复干净的透射内容；3) S2R-Bench：首个视频反射去除基准，支持全参考评估和真实世界人类感知评估。

Result: 在S2R-Bench和多个公开图像基准上达到最先进性能，推理速度甚至快于非扩散基线，并验证了S2R-Synthesis的有效性。

Conclusion: 该闭环框架有效解决了视频反射去除的数据、模型和评估三大挑战，证明了物理驱动合成与扩散模型结合的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Videos captured through glass often contain reflections that degrade visual quality and interfere with downstream vision tasks. Although single-image reflection removal has been extensively studied, video reflection removal remains largely underexplored due to the lack of paired video data, temporally coherent removal models, and dedicated evaluation benchmarks. We present a closed-loop framework that unifies physics-grounded reflection simulation, diffusion-based video dereflection, and benchmark evaluation. Our S2R-Synthesis pipeline generates paired reflected and reflection-free videos by performing physics-grounded augmentation in the structure space and rendering realistic reflected videos with a trained video diffusion renderer; the augmentation models key glass-related effects including roughness-induced blur, thickness-induced ghosting, and reflectance variation. Based on the synthesized data, we introduce S2R-Removal, the first diffusion-based video reflection removal model, which adapts a pretrained video diffusion prior through reflection-aware latent adaptation and one-step pixel-geometric refinement, recovering the clean transmission in a single denoising step. We further build S2R-Bench, the first benchmark for video reflection removal, supporting both full-reference evaluation and real-world human perceptual assessment. Experiments on S2R-Bench and multiple public image benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and faster inference than even non-diffusion baselines, and validate the effectiveness of S2R-Synthesis. Project page: https://codingwzp.github.io/VideoDereflection_S2R.

</details>


### [19] [Repurposing RGB-based Foundation Model for Depth Estimation on Thermal Images Using Hierarchical Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11564)
*Jie Hong,Tingtian Li,Xuesong Li,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RGB-HS框架，利用RGB基础模型的分层监督来提升热像深度估计性能。该方法用基础模型替换热像编码器，并引入平行的RGB分支（使用同架构的基础模型），在多个层级对两个编码器的token进行对齐，使热像学生分支能从RGB教师分支学习结构精度和语义抽象。同时引入基于RGB图像质量的验证机制来优化对齐过程。实验表明该方法在基准上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热像深度估计在恶劣环境（夜间、雨天）下对机器人应用极具价值，但现有方法在从RGB基础模型向热模态迁移知识时，未能充分利用这些模型编码的丰富分层表征。

Method: 1) 用基础模型替换基线热像编码器；2) 引入平行RGB分支，采用同架构的基础模型作为编码器；3) 在两个编码器的token之间进行多层级对齐，使热像学生分支捕获结构精度和语义抽象；4) 引入验证机制，根据RGB图像质量对token加权，优化对齐过程。

Result: 在公开基准上的广泛实验表明，RGB-HS在热像深度估计上取得了有竞争力的性能，并更有效地利用了RGB基础模型的表征能力。

Conclusion: RGB-HS通过分层监督和验证机制，证明了利用RGB基础模型的分层表征可以显著提升热像深度估计的性能，为跨模态知识迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: Depth estimation from thermal images is highly valuable for robotic applications in adverse conditions, such as nighttime and rainy weather. Recent studies have sought to transfer knowledge from RGB-based foundation models to thermal modalities, yet the rich hierarchical representations these models encode remain underutilized. To address this limitation, we propose RGB-HS, a novel framework for thermal-image depth estimation that leverages hierarchical supervision from an RGB-based foundation model. Specifically, we first replace the baseline thermal encoder with a foundational model and introduce a parallel RGB branch that also employs a foundational model as an encoder of the same architecture, taking RGB images as input. The alignment is then performed across multiple levels between the tokens of the two encoders, allowing the thermal student branch to capture both structural precision and semantic abstraction from the RGB teacher branch. Furthermore, we introduce verification to refine the alignment process by weighting tokens from the RGB branch based on RGB image quality. Extensive experiments on the popular benchmark demonstrate that RGB-HS achieves competitive performance and more effectively exploits the representational capacity of RGB-based foundation models for depth estimation on thermal images.

</details>


### [20] [Hand Visibility Detector: Per-Keypoint Visibility Estimation for Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11574)
*Ryosei Hara,Masashi Hatano,Rintaro Yanagi,Atsushi Hashimoto,Takuma Yagi,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将手部关节可见性估计作为一个独立任务进行系统研究，提出Hand Visibility Detector模型，利用大规模预训练的HPE模型作为骨干网络，并展示其在3D手部姿态标注下游任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部姿态估计（HPE）在AR/VR和机器人等应用中至关重要，关节可见性对遮挡条件下的估计可靠性评估十分关键。然而现有HPE方法大多只输出关节位置而不显式指示可见性，仅有的可见性估计研究也是作为辅助信号使用，缺乏对逐关节可见性估计作为独立任务的系统研究。

Method: 提出Hand Visibility Detector模型，用于估计单个手部关节的可见性；利用在大规模数据上预训练的HPE模型作为骨干网络，借助其先验知识提升可见性估计性能。

Result: 证明了利用大规模预训练HPE模型作为骨干网络在可见性估计任务上能获得高性能；在下游的3D手部姿态标注任务中（多视角2D关键点三角化），可见性加权的三角化方法有效降低了重投影误差。

Conclusion: 逐关节手部可见性估计可作为独立任务有效解决，且借助预训练HPE模型的先验知识能获得良好性能；该方法已作为可直接使用的软件包发布，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Hand Pose Estimation (HPE) is a fundamental technology for various applications such as AR/VR and robotics. In these applications, the visibility of each hand joint in the image is crucial for assessing the reliability of estimation results under occlusion. However, most existing HPE methods output joint positions without explicitly indicating their visibility. Although some methods account for occlusion or visibility, visibility estimation has mainly been used as an auxiliary signal for improving pose estimation. To our knowledge, per-joint hand visibility estimation has not been systematically studied as a standalone task. In this work, we propose Hand Visibility Detector, a model for estimating the visibility of individual hand joints, and present the first systematic investigation of visibility estimation as an independent task. We show that leveraging the prior knowledge of HPE models pretrained on large-scale data as a backbone yields high performance in this task. We further demonstrate the utility of Hand Visibility Detector on a downstream task of 3D hand pose annotation via multi-view triangulation of 2D keypoints, showing that visibility-weighted triangulation reduces reprojection error. Our method is released as a ready-to-use package, and the code and demo are available at https://github.com/ryhara/hand_visibility_detector .

</details>


### [21] [A Hybrid Framework of Vision Transformer and Gated Recurrent Unit for Detection of Mosquito Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11582)
*Danial Sharifrazi,Saadat Behzadi,Nouman Javed,Roohallah Alizadehsani,Prasad N. Paradkar,Asim Bhatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三步骤框架用于识别登革热病毒感染蚊虫与对照组蚊虫的运动行为差异：首先使用YOLO 11M模型识别蚊虫并去除背景，其次利用Vision Transformer (ViT)提取视觉特征，最后通过卷积GRU (ConvGRU)分类器对视频进行分类。实验表明ConvGRU模型表现最佳，达到88.88%准确率、84.45%精确率、82.82%召回率和82.81%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于蚊虫体积小且视频背景复杂，从视频中区分登革热病毒感染蚊虫与对照组蚊虫的运动行为十分困难。传统AI方法难以从视频帧中提取准确特征，容易产生错误特征，亟需更有效的分析框架。

Method: 采用三步骤框架：(1) 使用YOLO 11M模型识别蚊虫并移除背景；(2) 利用Vision Transformer (ViT)提取视觉特征；(3) 使用ConvGRU分类器对视频进行分类。并与RNN、LSTM、GRU及其卷积版本进行了对比分析。

Result: ConvGRU模型在所有对比模型中表现最佳，取得了88.88%的准确率、84.45%的精确率、82.82%的召回率和82.81%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 将卷积模型与序列网络相结合（尤其是ConvGRU模型）能够同时提取蚊虫运动的精确空间特征和长期时间依赖关系，为复杂环境中蚊虫行为分析提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Identifying dengue virus-infected mosquitoes from control mosquitoes is a major challenge in analyzing mosquito locomotion behavior due to the small size and complexity of the video background. Conventional AI methods are often unable to extract accurate features from video frames and produce erroneous features. In this study, a three-step framework is introduced: first, mosquitoes are identified and the background is removed using the YOLO 11M model, then visual features are extracted using the Vision Transformer (ViT), and finally the videos are classified with a convolutional GRU (ConvGRU) classifier. A comparative analysis of different models, including Recurrent Neural Network (RNN), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), and their convolutional versions showed that the ConvGRU model achieved the best performance; it achieved 88.88% accuracy, 84.45% precision, 82.82% recall, and 82.81% F1 score. These results demonstrate that combining convolutional models with sequence-based networks, especially in the ConvGRU model, allows the simultaneous extraction of precise spatial features and long-term temporal dependencies from mosquito movements. Finally, the proposed framework provides a reliable solution for analyzing mosquito behavior in complex environments.

</details>


### [22] [ProtoHGF-Net: Prototype HyperGraph Fusion with Intra-modal Calibration for RGBT Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11595)
*Xiangqi Chen,Xiuling Zhang,Chengzhuan Yang,Li Zhao,Dawei Zhang,Yanchao Wang,Liyuan Chen,Hua Wang,Hao Peng,Zhonglong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProtoHGF-Net，一种原型级超图融合网络，将RGBT跨模态融合从密集的全分辨率交互范式重新定义为原型级语义交互，并配合教师掩码校准蒸馏策略抑制背景干扰，在DroneVehicle、DVTOD和FLIR数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGBT检测方法主要依赖全分辨率特征的密集跨模态交互，这不可避免地引入背景干扰，阻碍了目标相关表征的学习。作者希望摆脱这一范式，实现更有选择性、更目标聚焦的融合方式。

Method: 1) 原型超图融合（Prototype HyperGraph Fusion）：在紧凑的原型级语义空间中进行跨模态交互，实现目标相关原型间的选择性融合；2) 教师掩码校准蒸馏（Teacher-Mask Calibration Distillation）：利用模态专属教师和目标感知掩码在融合前校准模态特征，抑制背景主导响应，生成更聚焦的特征。

Result: 在DroneVehicle、DVTOD和FLIR数据集上分别达到85.9%、88.2%和79.1%的mAP50，均取得SOTA性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 原型级超图融合相比密集跨模态交互能更有效地减少背景干扰、提升目标相关表征的学习，验证了原型级语义交互范式在RGBT检测中的有效性。

Abstract: RGB-Thermal (RGBT) object detection enables robust perception in complex scenes by leveraging the complementary strengths of visible textures and thermal cues. However, existing methods mainly rely on dense cross-modal interactions over full-resolution features, which inevitably introduce background interference and hinder the learning of target-relevant representations. In this paper, we propose the Prototype HyperGraph Fusion Network (ProtoHGF-Net), a novel framework that redefines cross-modal fusion as prototype-level semantic interaction rather than the dense cross-modal interaction paradigm. Specifically, we design Prototype HyperGraph Fusion to perform cross-modal interaction in a compact prototype-level semantic space. This design enables more selective fusion among target-relevant prototypes. To support this prototype-level fusion, we propose Teacher-Mask Calibration Distillation, which calibrates modality features before fusion using modality-specific teachers and target-aware masks. This strategy suppresses backgrou- nd-dominant responses and produces more target-focused features. Extensive experiments on DroneVehicle, DVTOD, and FLIR demonstrate that ProtoHGF-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance with 85.9\% $mAP_{50}$, 88.2\% $mAP_{50}$, and 79.1\% $mAP_{50}$, respectively. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/ZiMo-Chen/ProtoHGF}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [23] [How Can Driving World Models Do Counterfactual Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11601)
*Jiaru Zhang,Can Cui,Yi Xu,Xin Ye,Ruqi Zhang,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出驾驶世界模型在反事实预测任务中存在一个根本性缺陷：直接动作条件预测忽略了事实延续（factual continuation），导致无法保真地回答"如果采取其他动作会发生什么"这一问题。作者通过因果推理框架（abduction-action-prediction）形式化此差距，构建对照模拟基准进行验证，并提出一个无需训练的简单管线，将观测到的证据引入反事实视角，显著提升了反事实匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型被广泛用作反事实模拟器：给定真实驾驶日志，回答"在替代自我行为下会发生什么"。然而，直接的动作条件预测使用共享历史与替代动作，但忽略了该历史之后实际发生的事实延续，因此可能生成合理但与该情景不符的未来。这一"事实保真"缺口从未被系统性地形式化和量化。

Method: 1) 用因果推理的abduction-action-prediction框架形式化反事实预测与直接条件预测之间的差距；2) 在短时间范围内构建受控模拟基准，其中替代自我动作不改变周围智能体演化，从而提供可匹配的事实/反事实真值对；3) 在两个代表性世界模型上量化直接预测的失败；4) 提出一个简单、无需训练的管线，将观测证据移入反事实视角，让冻结模型补全剩余未知部分。

Result: 在两个代表性世界模型上，直接预测无法匹配反事实地面真值，证实了分析的正确性。简单的训练无关管线在两个模型上均显著提高了恢复比例（overall recovered fraction），并降低了与匹配反事实的感知距离。

Conclusion: 直接动作条件预测与反事实预测之间存在本质且可测量的差距。作者希望通过形式化该缺口并展示即使简单构造也能改善，引起社区对驾驶世界模型反事实预测方法的关注，推动更优方法的发展。

Abstract: Driving world models are often interpreted as counterfactual simulators for observed driving episodes: given a factual driving log, they are asked what would have happened under an alternative ego action. In this paper, we identify a fundamental mismatch between this goal and direct action-conditioned prediction. The direct prediction uses the shared history and the alternative action but not the factual continuation observed after that history. It can therefore generate a plausible future without preserving what actually happened in this episode. We formalize this gap using the causal recipe of abduction, action, and prediction and study it in a setting with a short time horizon, where the alternative ego action does not alter how surrounding agents evolve. To make the gap measurable, we construct a controlled simulation benchmark with factual outcomes and matched counterfactual outcomes. Across two representative world models, direct predictions fail to match the counterfactual ground truth, supporting our analysis. As a constructive check of this analysis, we introduce a deliberately simple, training-free pipeline that moves observed evidence into the counterfactual view and lets the frozen model complete what remains unknown. Even this simple construction raises the overall recovered fraction substantially and reduces perceptual distance to the matched counterfactual on both models. We hope this work draws attention to this gap and motivates better counterfactual prediction methods for driving world models.

</details>


### [24] [Topology-Aware Query Selection for Surgical Instrument Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11607)
*Ze Zhang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑感知的查询选择方法，将固定Mask2Former产生的非空候选实例表示为完整图，通过学习关系候选和成对表示、预测集合基数并求解结构化子集问题，以解决前景掩码虽准确但实例集存在重复、碎片化、合并、遗漏等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的前景掩码仍可能产生错误的器械实例集合（重复、碎片、合并、遗漏或空帧预测），这些错误在保持像素重叠的情况下仍违反对象身份和计数。最终查询选择是一个关系型的、可变基数的结构化问题，而非独立候选决策的集合。

Method: 将固定Mask2Former的非空候选表示为完整图，学习关系候选表示和成对表示，预测集合基数，并解决精确的结构化子集问题。正式对比包括完整关系路径与节点特征匹配路径，评估成对几何、消息传递和额外关系路径容量的综合效应。

Result: 在封闭的22例源测试中，三个发现种子均支持实例集性能提升：实例F1提升0.0504-0.0612，正帧集合失败率降低0.0848-0.1060。ROBUST-MIPS直接迁移在三个种子中完全复现结果；Endoscapes仅支持三个种子中的一个，未能建立稳定的直接迁移。

Conclusion: 结果支持有界结论：所评估的完整路径在指定原生实例契约下改进了固定Mask2Former候选的连贯实例集构建，但稳定的跨域迁移和组件特定效应尚未确立。

Abstract: Accurate foreground masks can still form an incorrect surgical-instrument instance set: duplicate, fragmented, merged, missed, or empty-frame predictions may preserve favorable pixel overlap while violating object identity and count. Final query selection is therefore a relational, variable-cardinality problem rather than a collection of independent candidate decisions. We evaluate topology-aware query selection, which represents the nonempty candidates of a fixed Mask2Former as a complete graph, learns relational candidate and pair representations, predicts set cardinality, and solves an exact structured subset problem. The formal comparison is the complete relational path versus a node-feature-matched path; it evaluates the combined effect of pairwise geometry, message passing, and the additional relational-path capacity, not an isolated component. On the sealed 22-case source test, all three discovery seeds supported instance-set performance improvement with segmentation fidelity and predefined technical-safety preservation: instance F1 increased by 0.0504--0.0612 and positive-frame set-failure rate decreased by 0.0848--0.1060. Direct ROBUST-MIPS transfer reproduced the complete result in all three seeds. Endoscapes supported only one of three seeds and therefore did not establish stable direct transfer. Taken together, the results support a bounded conclusion: the evaluated complete path improved coherent instance-set construction from fixed Mask2Former candidates in specified native-instance contracts, while stable cross-domain transfer and component-specific effects remain unestablished.

</details>


### [25] [KANResDiff: Learning Local Residual Diffusion via Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Ambiguous Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11617)
*Fanding Li,Chenglin Wang,Xiangyu Li,Xingyu Qiu,Xinghua Ma,Xiangming Yin,Haiyang Li,Suyu Dong,Wei Wang,Kuanquan Wang,Gongning Luo,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KANResDiff，一种用于模糊医学图像分割的新方法，通过结合Kolmogorov-Arnold网络学习局部残差扩散，为不同阶段分配不同的语义建模角色。实验在两个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊医学图像分割方法以固定且预定义的方式引入随机性，无法形成渐进的语义建模过程，缺乏灵活的阶段感知建模能力。

Method: 1) 提出独立时间编码（Independent Time Encoding），用基于样条的时间嵌入替代MLP的线性嵌入，增强推理阶段间的独立性并为不同阶段分配渐进语义角色。2) 提出残差薛定谔桥（Residual Schrodinger Bridge），通过构造局部薛定谔桥以可学习权重注入确定性残差先验，实现灵活的确定性-随机性交互和阶段感知的模糊建模。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，KANResDiff在GED和HM-IoU指标上达到SOTA，最大提升分别为16.8%和7.7%，同时在MDM指标上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: KANResDiff通过局部残差扩散和独立时间编码，成功实现了渐进式、阶段感知的模糊建模，显著优于现有方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Ambiguous medical image segmentation aims to provide a series of diverse but plausible segmentation hypotheses. However, existing methods introduce stochasticity in a fixed and pre-defined manner, failing to form a progressive semantic modeling process. To address these challenges, we propose KANResDiff to learn local residual diffusion with Kolmogorov-Arnold Network, thereby assigning distinct roles across stages for ambiguity modeling. Specifically, we propose Independent Time Encoding that offers spline-based time embeddings instead of linear ones from MLPs, which enhances the independence across inference stages and assigns progressive semantic roles to different stages. We propose Residual Schrodinger Bridge that injects deterministic residual prior with learnable weights by constructing local Schrodinger Bridge instead of following manually settings, achieving a flexible deterministic-stochastic interaction and stage-aware ambiguity modeling thanks to local optimal diffusion path. Extensive experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that KANResDiff achieves SOTA performance on GED and HM-IoU, with maximum improvements of 16.8% and 7.7%, respectively, while maintaining competitive performance on the MDM metric. Source code is available at https://github.com/PerceptionComputingLab/KANResDiff.

</details>


### [26] [Generative Video Compression Based on Hierarchical Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11618)
*Daowen Li,Ding Ding,Zifu Zhang,Kai Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GVCHR，一种基于分层参考的生成式视频压缩方法。其核心思想是将潜在帧按层次组织，使高质量参考帧同时服务于潜在编码和生成重建，显著提升编码效率和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的生成式视频压缩方法存在两方面局限：一是在潜在编码阶段未精心设计参考和质量结构；二是未考虑帧级质量变化对去噪过程的影响。这导致编码效率受限，并在生成重建过程中加剧伪影传播。

Method: GVCHR采用分层参考机制组织潜在帧。(1) 在潜在编码中，将分层参考结构与分层质量结构耦合，为被更频繁复用的低层帧分配更多比特，并引入分层时序上下文挖掘（Hierarchical Temporal Context Mining）利用长短时序上下文进行有效编码。(2) 在生成重建中，将编码端的分层结构嵌入分层注意力适配器（Hierarchical Attentive Adapter），附着于视频扩散Transformer上，通过分层注意力限制每帧只关注同层或更低层参考，从而减少去噪过程中的伪影传播。

Result: 在多个基准上验证，相比此前最先进方法，GVCHR在LPIPS和DISTS指标上分别取得50.5%和54.0%的BD-rate增益，并显著提升视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过分层参考组织潜在帧，兼顾编码效率与生成重建质量，验证了分层结构在生成式视频压缩中的有效性，为潜在帧组织与扩散去噪的协同设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative video compression has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve perceptual quality, where latent frames are required to be encoded efficiently while serving as denoising conditions. However, existing methods neither carefully design reference and quality structures during latent coding nor account for the impact of frame-level quality variation on denoising procedure, which limits coding efficiency and aggravates artifact propagation during generative reconstruction. In this paper, we propose GVCHR, Generative Video Compression based on Hierarchical Referencing. The key idea is to organize latent frames hierarchically, where the selected high-quality references benefit both latent coding and generative reconstruction. In latent coding, GVCHR couples a hierarchical reference structure with a hierarchical quality structure, assigning more bits to lower-layer frames that are reused more frequently as references. Built on this design, we introduce Hierarchical Temporal Context Mining to exploits complementary short- and long-term temporal context for effective latent coding. In generative reconstruction, the coding-side hierarchy is incorporated into a Hierarchical Attentive Adapter which is attached to a video diffusion transformer. This adapter uses hierarchical attention to restrict each latent frame to attend only to the same- or lower-layer references, thereby reducing artifact propagation during denoising. Experiments validate GVCHR on multiple benchmarks. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, GVCHR achieves 50.5% and 54.0% BD-rate gains in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, respectively, while also delivering clearly improved visual quality.

</details>


### [27] [CAM-Guided Saliency Cutout and Image-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11634)
*Yasaman Ebrahimi,Martin Jurecek,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以通过HiResCAM改善cutout放置位置，对比了无cutout、随机cutout、低显著性cutout和高显著性cutout四种训练条件，在恶意软件图像（RawMal-TF）和自然图像（CIFAR-100）上进行实验。结果显示恶意软件图像上所有cutout方法均不如无cutout，而CIFAR-100上低显著性cutout略有提升，表明显著性引导的cutout价值具有领域依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cutout作为一种数据增强方法在CNN中用于减少过拟合，但cutout的放置位置通常是随机的。作者想探究是否可以通过HiResCAM（高分辨率类激活映射）来指导cutout的放置位置，从而提高其效果。此外，作者也希望检验恶意软件图像是否应被视为与自然图像等同处理。

Method: 在ResNet18基础上，设置四种训练条件（无cutout、随机cutout、低显著性cutout、高显著性cutout），在RawMal-TF恶意软件数据集（17个家族，每家族约1000样本）和CIFAR-100数据集上进行对比实验。测试不同cutout面积（5%、10%、20%、30%）和不同增强副本数（M=4和8），训练约100个epoch。

Result: RawMal-TF数据集中，三种cutout方法（随机、高显著性、低显著性）的效果都略差于无cutout。而在CIFAR-100数据集中，低显著性cutout带来了略微的性能提升。两者结果形成对比。

Conclusion: 显著性引导的cutout的价值取决于具体领域：在自然图像上有效，但在恶意软件图像上效果不佳。恶意软件图像不应被视为与自然图像等同，需要针对其特点设计专门的增强或正则化策略。

Abstract: Dropout regularization is commonly used to reduce overfitting by removing parts of a neural network during training. For Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), cutouts serve a somewhat analogous purpose. Cutouts can be implemented as data augmentation: the original training image is retained, and additional copies are created with regions removed. In this chapter, we test whether cutout placement can be improved by using High-Resolution Class Activation Mapping (HiResCAM). We compare four controlled training conditions: no cutout, standard random cutout, low-saliency cutout, and high-saliency cutout. We experiment using grayscale malware images from the RawMal-TF dataset (17 families with~1,000 samples per family), and for comparison to natural images, we experiment with the well-known CIFAR-100 dataset. All experiments are based on ResNet18 with~100 training epochs. For the cutout experiments, we test cutout areas of~5\%, 10\%, 20\%, and~30\%, and we consider~$M\in\{4,8}$ augmented copies per original training image. The RawMal-TF results are slightly worse for all three cutout cases (random, high and low saliency) as compared to no cutouts. In contrast, our CIFAR-100 experimental results improve slightly under low-saliency cutout. These results suggest that the value of saliency-guided cutout is domain dependent, and that malware images should not be treated as equivalent to natural images.

</details>


### [28] [Robustness of AI-Art Detectors under Generator Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11643)
*Shivank Singh Thakur,Meien Li,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI艺术检测器在生成器迁移（从基于U-Net的扩散模型到Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium新架构）下的鲁棒性问题，发现深度学习模型在分布内表现良好但在跨生成器零样本测试中显著退化，其中CLIP ViT-L/14表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型（尤其是扩散Transformer架构）发展迅速，生成的图像越来越难以与人类作品区分，引发了版权、虚假信息、欺诈等担忧。然而，大多数AI艺术检测器的训练和评估都基于同一生成器家族，对新架构的鲁棒性未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了基于SD3.5 Medium的艺术作品数据集（覆盖十种艺术风格），通过反向提示生成；对五个检测器在U-Net扩散模型艺术品上进行训练，并在SD3.5m数据集上进行零样本跨生成器评估；使用Grad-CAM分析误分类样本的激活模式。

Result: 深度学习模型在分布内表现强但在生成器迁移下退化，将许多SD3.5m图像误分类为人类作品，而人类假阳性率保持较低。CLIP ViT-L/14整体表现最佳；Grad-CAM显示假阴性样本的激活更弱且更弥散。

Conclusion: 当前AI艺术检测器存在明显的泛化差距，应将其作为分层防御体系的一个组成部分，而非依赖单一检测器，以应对快速演进的生成架构。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models have advanced rapidly, with modern Diffusion Transformer architectures producing images that are increasingly difficult to distinguish from human-created artwork. This development has raised significant concerns regarding copyright protection, misinformation, fraud, impersonation, and the authenticity of digital content. Most AI-art detectors are trained and evaluated on the same generator family, leaving robustness to newer architectures underexplored. In this chapter, we analyze generator shift based on a Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium (SD3.5m) artwork dataset spanning ten art styles through reverse prompting of held-out human artwork samples. Five detectors are trained on U-Net-based latent diffusion artwork and evaluated in a zero-shot cross-generator setting on the SD3.5m dataset. Deep learning models perform strongly in-distribution but degrade under generator shift, misclassifying many SD3.5m images as human while human false positives remain low. The CLIP ViT-L/14 model performs best overall, while Grad-CAM analysis reveals weaker and more diffuse activation on false negatives. These findings highlight a generalization gap in current AI-art detectors and motivate the development of detectors as one component of a layered defense that remains reliable across rapidly evolving generative architectures.

</details>


### [29] [Cloak of Invisibility: Real-Time Privacy-Preserving Volumetric Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11645)
*Hossein Khalili,Philip Do,Alexander Vilesov,Kittipat Apicharttrisorn,Nader Sehatbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InViStream是一个面向体积视频流（Volumetric Video Streaming）的实时"源头隐私保护"系统，解决了多视角RGB-D融合场景中的隐私泄露问题。它通过目标检测与深度感知掩码相结合、跨校准视角传播公共/私有决策、仅融合消毒后的点云三个核心策略，在合成和真实场景中实现了高分割精度（Dice/Recall达到0.79-0.91）和超过30 FPS的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积视频流将隐私问题从传统的单帧二维问题扩展为三维多视角问题。在RGB-D体积管线中，多相机捕获的人、房间和个人物品会被融合成共享的三维表示，单个视角中遗漏或未完全移除的私有对象会在重建场景中重新出现。现有体积流系统主要优化重建、数据传输和延迟，而隐私保护视觉方法仅针对单相机单帧图像设计，无法直接处理校准的多视角RGB-D融合场景，因此需要专门针对该设置设计的源头隐私保护系统。

Method: InViStream采用"隐私从源头开始"的设计理念，在视觉和几何数据离开相机侧之前移除私有内容。系统解决三个挑战：（1）私有对象在不同视角中外观不同，采用目标检测结合深度感知掩码来处理；（2）仅RGB掩码会导致深度几何信息的隐私泄露，因此将掩码扩展至深度空间；（3）同一类别的公共/私有实例需要在云端融合前保持一致分离，通过跨校准视角传播公共/私有决策实现。最终仅融合经过消毒处理的点云。

Result: 在合成和真实RGB-D场景（包括办公室、会议室、客厅以及包含多个公共/私有人员和物体的场景）中验证：合成数据Dice/Recall为0.799/0.891，真实数据Dice/Recall为0.792/0.908，合成数据SSIM超过0.98，流式传输达到实时性能（超过30 FPS）。

Conclusion: InViStream证明了在体积视频流中实现源头级隐私保护是可行的，能够在保证实时性能（>30 FPS）和高重建质量（SSIM>0.98）的同时，有效地从多视角RGB-D融合管线中移除私有内容，填补了体积流系统在隐私保护方面的空白。

Abstract: Volumetric video streaming turns privacy into a 3D, multi-view problem. Unlike ordinary video, where sensitive content can often be redacted frame by frame, RGB-D volumetric pipelines capture people, rooms, and personal objects from multiple cameras and fuse them into a shared 3D representation. A private object missed in one view, or only partially removed before fusion, can therefore reappear in the reconstructed scene. This creates a privacy challenge for 3D telepresence, education, entertainment, and immersive applications: private content should be removed before raw visual and geometric data leave the camera side, while the public part of the scene should remain useful for real-time reconstruction. Existing volumetric streaming systems mainly optimize reconstruction, data movement, and latency, while privacy-preserving vision methods are designed for single-camera, single-frame images and do not directly address calibrated multi-view RGB-D fusion. We present InViStream, a real-time "privacy-from-source" system designed for this setting. InViStream addresses three challenges in volumetric capture: private objects may appear differently across views, RGB masking alone can leave geometric privacy leakage in depth, and public/private instances of the same class must be separated consistently before cloud-side fusion. To address these challenges, InViStream combines object detection with depth-aware masking, propagates public/private decisions across calibrated views, and fuses only sanitized point clouds. We evaluate InViStream on synthetic and real RGB-D scenes, including offices, conference rooms, living rooms, and settings with multiple public and private people and objects. InViStream achieves synthetic Dice/Recall of 0.799/0.891 and real Dice/Recall of 0.792/0.908, with synthetic SSIM above 0.98 and real-time streaming above 30 FPS.

</details>


### [30] [Hybrid-LUT: Channel-Aware Hybrid Lookup Table and Filtering for Efficient Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11646)
*Zhilin Ai,Boyu Li,Sidi Yang,Wenqing Shi,Wenyong Zhou,Binxiao Huang,Chenchen Ding,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid-LUT，一种基于YUV色彩空间的不对称通道处理框架，结合LUT和轻量滤波进行图像去噪。对Y通道使用多频段LUT分支恢复细节纹理，对UV通道使用轻量滤波保持色彩一致性，相比RGB-LUT方法将存储减少三分之二，同时保持相同吞吐量，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能（仅421 KB存储）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-LUT去噪方法需要三个相同LUT并行处理RGB通道，导致片上SRAM消耗大。简单地在YUV空间仅对亮度通道使用LUT会忽略色度通道，造成颜色失真和残余伪影。因此需要一种既能减少存储又能保持恢复质量的方案。

Method: 提出YUV-based不对称通道处理框架Hybrid-LUT：1）对Y（亮度）通道采用多频段LUT分支并融合像素级权重，以恢复精细纹理；2）对UV（色度）通道采用轻量滤波，以维持颜色一致性。整体设计将LUT和滤波统一到同一框架中。

Result: 相比RGB-LUT方法，LUT存储减少三分之二且吞吐量相当。仅需421 KB存储即达到SOTA性能，在真实世界数据集上相比现有LUT去噪方法CPSNR至少提升0.63 dB。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LUT通过YUV色彩空间下的非对称通道处理，在显著减少存储开销的同时保持甚至超越现有LUT方法的去噪质量，适用于资源受限的边缘设备上的图像去噪应用。

Abstract: Lookup table (LUT)-based image denoising methods have attracted increasing attention due to their high efficiency and hardware-friendly properties. However, existing RGB-LUT approaches require three identical LUTs to process RGB channels in parallel, resulting in large on-chip SRAM consumption. A simple alternative is to apply LUT processing only to the luminance (Y) channel in the YUV color space to reduce memory usage. However, this naive strategy leads to degraded restoration quality, since ignoring the chrominance (UV) channels introduces color distortion and residual artifacts. In this work, we propose Hybrid-LUT, a YUV-based asymmetric channel-processing framework that combines LUT and filtering in a unified design. Specifically, a multi-band LUT branch with pixel-level weight fusion is applied to the Y channel to recover fine textures, while lightweight filtering is used for the UV channels to maintain color consistency. This design reduces LUT storage by two-thirds compared with RGB-LUT methods while maintaining the same runtime throughput. Extensive experiments show that Hybrid-LUT achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across multiple benchmarks with only 421 KB of storage. In particular, our method surpasses existing LUT-based denoising approaches by at least 0.63 dB CPSNR on real-world datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness for image denoising on resource-constrained edge devices. The project is available at https://github.com/Ai-ZL/Hybrid-LUT .

</details>


### [31] [Motion-as-Prompt: Enhancing Motion Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models via Motion-Guided Cross-Frame Visual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11655)
*Xikai Sun,Kebin Liu,Haotian Wang,Li Liu,Xu Wang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Motion-as-Prompt (MaP)框架，通过稠密点轨迹引导的跨帧视觉提示，将运动信息直接标记到视频帧上，使冻结的多模态大语言模型能够感知帧间的位移、方向变化和交互，无需训练或修改架构即可提升运动推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通常通过稀疏均匀采样处理视频以控制视觉令牌和注意力成本，但这种方式可能丢失采样帧之间的关键过渡信息，从而限制了对物体运动、碰撞和因果交互的推理能力。

Method: MaP框架包含三个步骤：1)恢复稠密点轨迹；2)选择运动信息丰富的帧；3)将连续采样帧之间累积的轨迹直接标记到视觉输入上，使原本隐藏的位移、方向变化和交互对冻结的MLLM可见。

Result: 在CLEVRER和Something-Something-v2数据集上，MaP在GPT-5.5上分别提升了4.2%和8.9%的平均运动推理准确率，且不损失非运动理解能力。

Conclusion: MaP提供了一种简单有效的解决方案，无需模型训练或架构修改即可增强运动中心的视频推理，具有较好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Motion-centric video reasoning is fundamental to interactive applications such as robotic manipulation and autonomous navigation. However, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically process videos through sparse uniform sampling to control visual-token and attention costs. This strategy may discard critical transitions between sampled frames, limiting reasoning about object movement, collisions, and causal interactions. To mitigate this issue, we propose Motion-as-Prompt (MaP), a track-guided cross-frame visual prompting framework. MaP recovers dense point trajectories, selects motion-informative frames, and marks the trajectories accumulated between consecutive sampled frames directly onto the visual inputs, making otherwise hidden displacement, direction changes, and interactions observable to frozen MLLMs. Experiments on CLEVRER and Something-Something-v2 show that MaP consistently improves average motion-reasoning accuracy, yielding gains of 4.2% and 8.9% for GPT-5.5, respectively. Notably, these improvements are obtained without degrading non-motion understanding, highlighting the robustness of MaP. These results demonstrate that MaP provides a simple and effective solution for enhancing motion-centric video reasoning without model training or architectural modification. Project page:https://github.com/SunVictor23/MaP.

</details>


### [32] [Zero-OVCD: Bridging Training-Free Foundation Models and Pseudo-Label Learning for Open-Vocabulary Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11663)
*Daifeng Peng,Yuanke Peng,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-OVCD，一个无需目标域像素级标注的两阶段开放词汇变化检测框架。第一阶段通过互补候选掩膜精炼、多尺度语义相似度融合与基于边距的可靠性过滤、以及响应引导的掩膜修正与补全，生成高质量变化伪标签；第二阶段利用伪标签训练变化检测器，并通过检查点投票和高一致性样本选择缓解残差伪标签噪声。在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、S2Looking和SECOND数据集上均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）的无训练流程存在三个核心缺陷：候选掩膜不准确、语义分配模糊、推理错误累积。这些问题严重制约了OVCD在无目标域标注场景下的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出两阶段Zero-OVCD框架：阶段一生成高质量变化伪标签，包括(1)互补候选掩膜精炼以抑制噪声候选；(2)多尺度语义相似度融合配合基于边距的可靠性过滤以增强掩膜级语义判别；(3)响应引导的掩膜修正与补全以恢复遗漏的变化区域。阶段二用伪标签训练变化检测器，引入检查点投票和高一致性样本选择来抑制残差伪标签噪声。

Result: LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和S2Looking上，Stage I的F1分别为86.25%、85.82%和50.48%，Stage II进一步提升至88.65%、88.85%和57.96%；SECOND上六类一对余任务的宏观平均F1从47.91%提升至50.92%。

Conclusion: 桥接无训练的基础模型推理与噪声感知的伪标签学习，为无需目标域像素级标注的开放词汇变化检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary change detection (OVCD) enables the identification of user-specified land-cover changes in bitemporal remote sensing images, but existing training-free pipelines remain vulnerable to inaccurate candidate masks, ambiguous semantic assignments, and accumulated inference errors. To address these issues, we propose Zero-OVCD, a two-stage framework that requires no pixel-level annotations from the target domain. In the first stage, high-quality change pseudo-labels are generated through complementary candidate-mask refinement, multiscale semantic similarity fusion with margin-based reliability filtering, and response-guided mask correction and completion. These components jointly suppress noisy candidates, enhance mask-level semantic discrimination, and recover missed change regions. In the second stage, a change detector is trained using the generated pseudo-labels, while checkpoint voting and high-agreement sample selection are introduced to mitigate residual pseudo-label noise. On LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, and S2Looking, Stage I achieves F1 scores of 86.25%, 85.82%, and 50.48%, while Stage II further improves them to 88.65%, 88.85%, and 57.96%, respectively. On SECOND, the macro-average F1 across six category-wise one-vs-rest tasks increases from 47.91% to 50.92%. These results demonstrate that bridging training-free foundation-model inference with noise-aware pseudo-label learning provides an effective solution for open-vocabulary change detection without target-domain pixel-level annotations. Code will be available at https://github.com/1321663019/Zero-OVCD.

</details>


### [33] [Learning from Multimodal Pseudo-Labels for Robust Open-Vocabulary Instance and Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11681)
*Duy Tran Thanh,Yeejin Lee,Byeongkeun Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态框架,利用预训练视觉-语言模型(Grounded SAM、LLaVA、CLIP)自动生成伪标签和同义词集,并通过三种训练目标(扩展接地损失、语义一致性损失、生成式标题重建损失)增强视觉-文本对齐,在开放词汇实例分割(OVIS)和开放集全景分割(OSPS)任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇实例分割和开放集全景分割面临三大挑战:伪掩码噪声大、视觉-文本接地有限、难以处理同义词和词汇外(OOV)单词。现有方法依赖大量人工标注,难以识别未见过的类别。

Method: (1) 利用Grounded SAM生成伪分割掩码,LLaVA生成描述性标题,CLIP进行同义词筛选,构建无需人工标注的多模态监督;(2) 提出三种互补训练目标:扩展接地损失(融入视觉接地同义词)、语义一致性损失、生成式标题重建损失,共同增强视觉-文本对齐。

Result: 在COCO数据集上的实验表明,该方法在OVIS和OSPS基准上均持续超越现有最先进方法,取得显著改进。

Conclusion: 多模态伪标签生成与同义词感知的训练策略能有效解决开放词汇分割中的噪声标签和语义对齐问题,展示了利用大规模预训练模型替代人工标注的可行性。

Abstract: This work addresses the challenge of open-vocabulary instance segmentation (OVIS) and open-set panoptic segmentation (OSPS), which aim to recognize both predefined and unseen object categories without exhaustive human annotations. Existing methods often suffer from noisy pseudo-masks, limited visual-textual grounding, and difficulty handling synonyms or out-of-vocabulary (OOV) words. To overcome these challenges, we propose a multimodal framework that leverages pre-trained vision-language models for automatic pseudo-label generation, CLIP-guided synonym filtering, and GPT-based caption reconstruction. In our target-vocabulary-assisted pseudo-labeling setting, the framework first constructs pseudo segmentation masks, descriptive captions, and semantically aligned synonym sets using Grounded SAM, LLaVA, and CLIP, providing multimodal supervision without manual annotation. We then enhance visual-textual alignment through three complementary training objectives: an extended grounding loss that incorporates visually grounded synonyms, a semantic consistency loss, and a generative caption reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments on the COCO dataset demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches under this protocol, achieving substantial improvements on both OVIS and OSPS benchmarks.

</details>


### [34] [EGM-Det: Entropy-Guided Multimodal Adaptive Fusion for UAV RGB-IR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11685)
*Cunzheng Fan,Dawei Yan,Guanlin Wang,Xingshuo Yang,Yupeng Jia,Jing Yang,Haokui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EGM-Det，一种基于熵引导的多模态自适应融合框架，用于无人机视角下的RGB-红外目标检测。通过熵偏移门控融合模块实现空间变化的模态可靠性感知融合，并在三个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RGB-IR多模态融合方法使用静态或固定权重融合多模态特征，忽略了不同空间位置上模态可靠性的变化，导致融合效果不佳。

Method: 1) 采用双流架构保留模态特定表征；2) 提出熵偏移门控融合模块（Entropy Offset Gate Fusion），从输入强度、局部熵和跨模态差异中提取浅层熵先验，用于引导局部偏移对齐和空间-通道门控融合；3) 引入跨模态蒸馏以正则化学习到的融合门控，减少融合退化，每个学生分支从面向主分支的跨模态教师分支提取互补知识。

Result: 在DroneVehicle、LLVIP和VEDAI三个基准上均取得SOTA性能，在VEDAI上比先前方法提升超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 熵引导的自适应融合能有效感知空间变化的模态可靠性，选择性聚合RGB和红外线索，显著提升无人机视角目标检测性能。

Abstract: Joint use of RGB and infrared (IR) imagery can improve UAV-view object detection, but most existing methods fuse multimodal features with static or fixed weights and therefore overlook spatially varying modality reliability. We propose EGM-Det, an entropy-guided multimodal adaptive fusion framework for RGB-IR object detection. EGM-Det employs a dual-stream architecture to preserve modality-specific representations and introduces an Entropy Offset Gate Fusion module for adaptive multi-scale fusion. The module derives shallow entropy priors from input intensity, local entropy, and cross-modal discrepancy, and uses them to guide local offset alignment and spatial-channel gated fusion. It therefore selectively aggregates reliable RGB and infrared cues instead of uniformly combining heterogeneous features. We further introduce cross-modal distillation to regularize the learned fusion gates and reduce fusion degradation. Each student branch extracts complementary knowledge from the cross-modality teacher branch matched to the main branch, while entropy-adaptive supervision emphasizes uncertain modality decisions. Experiments on DroneVehicle, LLVIP, and VEDAI demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all three benchmarks; in particular, EGM-Det outperforms prior approaches by more than 10 percentage points on VEDAI.

</details>


### [35] [Boundary-Enhanced Segmentation of Pig Point Clouds in Commercial Housing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11697)
*Zhankang Xu,Fei Shi,Xiangyu Qi,Zhaoyang Wang,Mengxin Guo,Yikai Fan,Simon X. Yang,Qifeng Li,Weihong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边界特征分析的猪点云分割方法，采用Octree Transformer作为骨干网络，结合软距离边界伪标签监督和双向跨边界语义模块，有效解决猪舍环境中点云边界模糊、局部粘连和背景误分割等问题，显著提升了分割精度和边界清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实猪舍环境中，猪点云与背景结构紧密接触，导致目标边界模糊、局部粘连和背景误分割，这会降低下游点云补全和体型测量的精度，亟需一种能够有效处理边界问题的分割方法。

Method: 以Octree Transformer为骨干网络，通过八叉树卷积、自注意力编码和多尺度特征融合整合局部几何细节与全局语义上下文；生成软距离边界伪标签提供连续的边界监督；设计双向跨边界语义模块实现边界特征与语义特征的显式交互。

Result: 在综合数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分割精度、平均交并比和边界描绘方面均显著优于多种最新模型，有效缓解了边界粘连问题。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效缓解点云边界粘连，为下游精准畜牧业任务（如点云补全和体型测量）提供可靠的点云输入。

Abstract: In real pigsty environments, pig point clouds often come into close contact with background structures, resulting in blurred target boundaries, local adhesion, and background mis-segmentation. This reduces the accuracy of subsequent point cloud completion and body size measurement. To address these challenges, this study proposes a pig point cloud segmentation method based on boundary feature analysis. The proposed method adopts Octree Transformer as the backbone network and integrates local geometric details with global semantic context through octree convolution, self-attention encoding, and multi-scale feature fusion. Furthermore, soft-distance boundary pseudo-labels are generated to provide continuous boundary supervision, and a bidirectional cross-boundary semantic module is designed to enable explicit interaction between boundary and semantic features. Experiments conducted on a comprehensive dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms various state-of-the-art models in terms of segmentation accuracy, mean intersection over union, and boundary delineation. The results indicate that the method effectively alleviates boundary adhesion, providing reliable point cloud inputs for downstream precision livestock farming tasks.

</details>


### [36] [STAR: A Spatial-Topology Aware Routing Framework for Generalizable 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11699)
*Mingwei Xing,Xinliang Wang,Yifeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR（空间-拓扑感知路由框架），用于构建统一的3D场景理解模型。通过多属性自监督预训练分支锚定跨域结构先验，并设计领域感知专家分支（包含DSR和EDA两种机制），解决不同传感器模态间的拓扑差异导致的专家分配困难问题。在ScanNet和S3DIS等室内外场景数据集上取得了显著效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一3D场景理解模型长期受限于不同传感器模态间的拓扑差异。虽然MoE架构是处理多域3D理解任务的灵活方案，但传统的特征-only MoE路由器在语义监督下可能无法充分表征局部采样拓扑结构，导致当语义一致性伴随几何异质性时专家分配变得困难。

Method: 提出STAR框架，包含两个核心分支：(1)多属性自监督预训练分支，覆盖拓扑和纹理变化，用于锚定跨域结构先验；(2)领域感知专家分支，包含两种机制——Domain-Spatial-Guided Routing (DSR)从空间上下文捕获局部拓扑变化，以及Entropy-controlled Dynamic Allocation (EDA)根据路由不确定性动态调整激活专家数量。两者结合实现稳定的跨域表示学习与自适应专家分配。

Result: 在多个室内外场景任务中验证了有效性：ScanNet验证集上达到80.1% mIoU，S3DIS上达到77.2% mIoU，一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将稳定的跨域表示学习与自适应的专家分配相结合，STAR有效解决了多模态3D理解中的拓扑差异问题，为统一的3D场景理解模型构建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Constructing a unified 3D scene understanding model has long been hindered by the topological discrepancies across sensor modalities. While applying the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is a flexible approach for multi-domain 3D understanding, we observe that conventional feature-only MoE routers may underrepresent local sampling topology under semantic supervision, making expert allocation difficult when semantic consistency coexists with geometric heterogeneity. To overcome this challenge, we propose STAR (Spatial-Topology Aware Routing Framework). Specifically, we introduce a multi-attribute self-supervised pre-training branch, covering topological and textural variations, to anchor cross-domain structural priors. Building upon this, we design a domain-aware expert branch with two mechanisms: Domain-Spatial-Guided Routing (DSR), which captures local topological variations from spatial context, and Entropy-controlled Dynamic Allocation (EDA), which adjusts the number of activated experts according to routing uncertainty. Together, these branches combine stable cross-domain representation learning with adaptive expert allocation. Extensive experiments across various tasks, encompassing both indoor and outdoor scenes, demonstrate the effectiveness of STAR. It achieves 80.1% mIoU on the ScanNet validation set and 77.2% mIoU on S3DIS, consistently improving over strong baselines. Code is available at our project page (https://xmw666.github.io/STAR/).

</details>


### [37] [Advancing MLLM-based UAV Image Understanding and Reasoning: A Benchmark and a Training-Free Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11738)
*Haoyu Zhang,Shuoxun Zhang,Peng Ye,Lin Zhang,Jiakang Yuan,Shenghong Yi,Yuening Wang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了UAVQA-Bench基准（含1,500个人工标注问答对，覆盖13个公开UAV数据集、6个能力维度和16个任务），系统评估了多种MLLM后识别出三个关键失败模式（领域工具集不匹配、未检查的错误传播和静态推理），并提出UAV-MAS多智能体系统（含DSPE、CAIR、DAAS三个模块）来应对这些挑战，在32B开源模型上达到77.0%的准确率，超越Gemini 3 Pro 4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在无人机航拍图像理解与推理方面缺乏统一评估框架，评估分散在单个数据集和狭窄任务上，无法全面衡量模型在极端尺度变化、任意相机方向和密集目标场景下的理解与推理能力，因此需要构建统一的基准和系统的解决方案。

Method: 首先构建UAVQA-Bench基准，从13个公开UAV数据集中人工标注1,500个QA对，覆盖6个能力维度和16个任务（含多项选择和视觉定位格式）。基于系统性评估发现三个关键失败模式后，提出UAV-MAS多智能体系统，包含三个核心模块：领域特定感知引擎（DSPE）将查询路由到合适的视觉工具、上下文感知迭代精炼模块（CAIR）验证中间推理以减少错误累积、难度感知自适应搜索机制（DAAS）根据问题难度调整搜索深度。

Result: UAV-MAS在32B开源MLLM上取得77.0%的总体准确率，超过Gemini 3 Pro 4.0%；8B版本相比基础模型提升了8.7%。

Conclusion: 通过统一基准UAVQA-Bench揭示了MLLM在UAV图像理解中的系统性缺陷，并验证了无需训练的轻量级多智能体架构（UAV-MAS）能有效提升现有MLLM的UAV图像理解与推理能力，为无人机视觉智能提供了可行的改进路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning is essential for aerial intelligence yet poses distinct challenges arising from extreme scale variation, arbitrary camera orientations, and high object density. Despite growing interest, existing evaluations remain fragmented across individual datasets and narrow tasks, leaving a critical gap in unified assessment of UAV understanding and reasoning capabilities. To fill this gap, we construct UAVQA-Bench, a benchmark of 1,500 human-annotated QA pairs drawn from 13 public UAV datasets, covering 6 capability dimensions and 16 tasks in both multiple-choice and visual grounding formats. Systematic evaluation of a broad range of open-source and closed-source MLLMs as well as agent-based systems on UAVQA-Bench identifies three key failure modes: domain-toolset mismatch, unchecked error propagation, and static reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose UAV-MAS, a training-free multi-agent system for MLLM-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning, comprising a Domain-Specific Perception Engine (DSPE) that routes queries to task-appropriate visual tools, a Context-Aware Iterative Refinement module (CAIR) that validates intermediate reasoning to curb error accumulation, and a Difficulty-Aware Adaptive Search mechanism (DAAS) that adjusts search depth to question difficulty. UAV-MAS with a 32B open-source MLLM achieves 77.0% overall accuracy on UAVQA-Bench, surpassing Gemini 3 Pro by 4.0\%, while the 8B variant improves 8.7\% over its base model.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古文字训诂需要整合视觉观察、语言学分析和历史语境，但现有计算方法仅聚焦于字符识别、检索等子任务，缺乏支持全面学术分析的标准化数据集和基准。

Method: 1) 提出ACCEE四层次VQA任务框架（识别→形态→释义→历时演变）；2) 通过专家设计模板+原文约束的生成管线构建JieZi-Dataset（超50万QA对），并在关键阶段进行人工验证；3) 构建由人类专家验证的JieZi-Bench评估基准，参考答案来自权威训诂著作且与训练数据隔离。

Result: 实验表明当前多模态大模型在基础字符识别表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理和历时理解上存在困难；在JieZi-Dataset上微调后，模型在四个层次上的表现均有显著提升。

Conclusion: ACCEE框架及其资源填补了古文字训诂计算分析领域的空白，为系统性古文字AI研究提供了训练数据和可靠评估基准，证明了针对下游任务微调的有效性。

Abstract: The scholarly exegesis of ancient Chinese characters demands integrating visual observation, linguistic analysis, and historical context. However, existing computational approaches focus narrowly on subtasks such as character recognition and retrieval, lacking the structured datasets and benchmarks required for comprehensive scholarly analysis. To address this limitation, we introduce Ancient Chinese Character Exegesis (ACCE), a vision-language question answering (VQA) task that models the scholarly exegesis process. ACCE is organized into four progressive levels: basic character identification, glyph-form analysis, meaning exegesis, and diachronic evolution analysis. To support this task, we construct two complementary resources. JieZi-Dataset is the first large-scale, expert-audited VQA training dataset for ACCE, comprising over 500K QA pairs. It is constructed via a pipeline that reduces factual errors by constraining generation with expert-designed templates and source-text references. Human verification is further applied at each key stage to ensure scholarly accuracy. JieZi-Bench is an evaluation benchmark aligned with the exegesis process, constructed and verified by human experts to ensure evaluation reliability. It consists of four levels with reference answers curated from authoritative lexicographic works held separate from the training data. Experiments on multimodal large language models show that current models perform well on basic identification but struggle with glyph analysis, semantic reasoning, and diachronic understanding. Fine-tuning on JieZi-Dataset substantially improves performance across all four levels. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Ran00w/JieZi.

</details>

*Yuxuan Zhang,Haozhong Xiong,Yubo Huang,Jiayi Song,Jinpeng Yu,Haofan Wang,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveAnimate提出了首个结合实时流式生成与稳定长时生成的十亿级参数人体动画系统，基于14B参数的视频DiT，通过两阶段训练（Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation和Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation）实现三步采样，并结合PR-Sink KV缓存机制在流式时长增长时保持恒定内存和延迟，在两张H100上达到19.63 FPS的流式推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在姿态驱动人体动画中虽然质量高，但生成速度极慢（每个片段需数分钟到数小时），无法满足实时交互应用（如直播、远程在场、虚拟化身）的需求。现有系统在长视频生成时面临质量退化或需要大量离线计算的问题。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 两阶段训练流程——先通过Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation将预训练的双向DiT适配为block-causal自回归生成器，再通过Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation将采样步数降至3步；(2) Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention (PR-Sink)——一种有界KV缓存机制，包含Static Sink（固定锚定第一个生成块）、Dynamic Sink（持有姿态检索的历史块）和三层滑动窗口，在姿态重复时恢复外观上下文而无需保留完整序列；(3) 结合Ulysses序列并行和算子融合优化推理。

Result: 在两张NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现19.63 FPS的流式推理。在三分钟基准测试中，LiveAnimate从最初30秒到最后一分钟保持近乎恒定的感知质量和身份一致性（IQA 4.047 vs 4.026），而现有系统在同等条件下质量显著下降或需要数小时的离线计算。

Conclusion: LiveAnimate在质量、延迟和时长三个维度上为交互式全身动画建立了新的工作点，证明了十亿级扩散模型可以在保持长时稳定性的同时实现实时流式生成，为实时交互式人体动画应用开辟了可能性。

Abstract: Pose-driven human animation synthesizes a video of a target person from a single reference image and a driving pose stream. Real-time generation is essential for interactive applications such as live streaming, telepresence, and virtual avatars, yet diffusion-based systems require minutes to hours per clip, precluding responsive interaction. We present LiveAnimate, to our knowledge the first animation system to combine real-time streaming with stable long-form generation at billion scale, built on a 14B-parameter video Diffusion Transformer (DiT). A two-stage training pipeline first adapts a pretrained bidirectional DiT into a block-causal autoregressive generator through Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation, and then reduces the sampling budget to three steps through Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation. To preserve appearance over extended streams, we introduce Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention (PR-Sink), a bounded KV-cache mechanism combining a Static Sink that permanently anchors the first generated block, a Dynamic Sink that holds a pose-retrieved historical block, and a three-slot Rolling Window. When a pose recurs, PR-Sink restores the relevant appearance context without retaining the entire sequence, so memory and per-block latency remain constant regardless of stream duration. Together with Ulysses sequence parallelism and operator fusion, these designs enable 19.63\,FPS streaming inference on two NVIDIA H100 GPUs. On a three-minute benchmark, LiveAnimate maintains nearly constant perceptual quality and identity from the first 30 seconds to the final minute (IQA 4.047 vs.\ 4.026), while prior systems degrade substantially or require hours of offline computation for the same rollout. These results establish a new operating point in quality, latency, and duration for interactive full-body animation.

</details>


### [40] [Making Every Step Count: Spatio-Temporal Information Allocation for Imaging Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11747)
*Yi Cao,Xiangyong Cao,Pei Liu,Yong-Jin Liu,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两个互补且免训练的组件（SAS和MPA），用于改善基于流的生成模型在无训练逆问题求解中的时空信息分配问题，可即插即用地集成到现有求解器中并显著提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的逆问题求解器主要关注单个步骤的设计，忽略了对固定NFEs下时空信息分配的考量。时间上，早期探索不足会陷入错误的语义区域，而早期分配过多则留给后期精修的预算不足；空间上，数据一致性只在观测区域提供约束，缺失区域的恢复主要依赖生成先验。这两个问题限制了求解质量。

Method: 提出两个互补且免训练的组件：(1) Spectrum-Adaptive Scheduling (SAS)——根据退化频谱和logSNR几何分布可用的NFEs，在语义探索和细节精修之间取得更好的平衡；(2) Measurement-Prioritized Attention (MPA)——利用数据-先验冲突引导信息流向弱约束区域，增强语义和结构保真度。两者均可即插即用地集成到现有基于流的逆求解器中。

Result: 在超分辨率、运动去模糊和图像修复等标准图像逆问题上，所提出的组件可以以即插即用的方式集成到现有基于流的逆求解器中，无需重新训练或额外的流模型评估，并显著提升了现有求解器的重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过显式地建模时空信息分配问题，SAS和MPA为基于流的无训练逆问题求解提供了一个简单而有效的通用改进方案，兼具可扩展性和实用价值。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have emerged as powerful image priors for training-free inverse problem solving, capturing coherent semantics and fine-grained structure. Despite these strengths, existing flow-based inverse solvers primarily focus on the design of individual updates, largely overlooking spatio-temporal information allocation under a fixed number of function evaluations (NFEs). Temporally, insufficient early exploration can trap the flow trajectory in an incorrect semantic basin, whereas excessive allocation of NFEs to early stages leaves little budget for late-stage refinement. Spatially, data consistency provides direct constraints only within observed regions, whereas the recovery of missing regions relies mainly on the generative prior. To address these two issues, we introduce two complementary and training-free components, i.e., Spectrum-Adaptive Scheduling (SAS) and Measurement-Prioritized Attention (MPA). For temporal allocation, SAS distributes the available NFEs over flow time according to the degradation spectrum and logSNR geometry, thus better balancing semantic exploration and detail refinement. For spatial propagation, MPA exploits data-prior conflicts to guide information toward weakly constrained regions, thereby enhancing semantic and structural fidelity. Extensive experiments on standard image inverse problems, e.g., super-resolution, motion deblurring, and inpainting, demonstrate that the proposed components can be integrated into existing flow-based inverse solvers in a plug-and-play manner without retraining or additional flow-model evaluations, and can also significantly improve the restoration quality of existing solvers.

</details>


### [41] [Dual Modality Prompted Diffusion Priors for Zero Shot Hyperspectral Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11748)
*Pengwei Xie,Fei Zhu,Jiajun Li,Xiangyuan Liu,Xiangyuan Liu,Kangqing Shen,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双模态图像提示的扩散模型（DIDM），用于零样本高光谱全色锐化。DIDM 将低分辨率高光谱图像和全色图像分别编码为光谱和空间提示令牌，通过交叉注意力注入冻结的遥感扩散模型中间特征，并引入全色引导的加权像素感知全变分正则化项，在三个数据集上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的高光谱全色锐化方法仅通过外部重建目标约束观测图像，限制了PAN和HSI图像与扩散先验的直接交互，无法充分利用互补的光谱与空间信息。

Method: 1) 设计双模态提示编码器，将LRHS和PAN分别编码为光谱和空间提示令牌；2) 通过交叉注意力机制将提示注入冻结的遥感扩散模型中间特征；3) 提出全色引导的加权像素感知全变分正则化，结合LRHS退化保真度与PAN响应保真度及梯度自适应结构化正则化。

Result: 在Pavia、Chikusei和Houston数据集的降分辨率协议下，DIDM在所有评估指标上取得最佳表现；在FR1上的全分辨率评估中获得最高的HQNR。

Conclusion: 内部双模态提示和全色引导的结构化正则化能够有效平衡空间细节增强与光谱保真度，验证了该方法的优越性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral pansharpening aims to reconstruct a high resolution hyperspectral (HRHS) image from a panchromatic (PAN) image and a low resolution hyperspectral (LRHS) image while preserving both spatial details and spectral fidelity. Recent diffusion based methods exploit pretrained image priors by generating a low dimensional representation and subsequently mapping it to the hyperspectral domain. However, the observed panchromatic and hyperspectral images are typically imposed only through external reconstruction objectives, limiting their direct interaction with the diffusion prior. To address this issue, we propose dual-modality image-prompted diffusion model (DIDM) for zero shot hyperspectral pansharpening. DIDM encodes the low resolution hyperspectral and panchromatic observations into spectral and spatial prompt tokens, respectively, and injects them into intermediate features of a frozen remote sensing diffusion model through cross attention, allowing complementary spectral and spatial information to directly guide diffusion feature evolution. In addition, we introduce a panchromatic guided weighted pixel aware total variation regularizer that combines low resolution hyperspectral degradation fidelity and panchromatic response fidelity with gradient adaptive structural regularization, thereby preserving structural discontinuities while suppressing spurious variations in homogeneous regions. Extensive experiments on Pavia, Chikusei, and Houston under reduced resolution protocols show that DIDM achieves the best performance across all evaluated metrics, while full resolution evaluation on FR1 yields the highest HQNR among the compared methods. These results demonstrate that internal dual modality prompting and panchromatic guided structural regularization provide an effective balance between spatial detail enhancement and spectral preservation.

</details>
